公司簡介
COMPANY PROFILE
農夫山泉股份有限公司成立於1996年，為 Established in 1996, Nongfu Springs Co., Ltd. is a leader in the
中國包裝飲用水及飲料的龍頭企業。我們的 packaged drinking water and beverage industry in the PRC. Our
產品主要覆蓋包裝飲用水、茶飲料、功能飲 major products include packaged drinking water, tea beverage,
料及果汁飲料等類別。本公司於2001年6月 functional beverage and juice beverage. The Company was
從有限責任公司整體變更為股份有限公司， transformed from a limited liability company into a joint stock
並於2020年9月在香港聯交所主板上市（股 company with limited liabilities in June 2001 and listed on the Main
份代號：9633. HK）。 Board of the Hong Kong Stock Exchange in September 2020 (stock
code: 9633. HK).
我們秉持「天然、健康」的產品理念，堅持使 We believe in offering natural and healthy products and insist on
用優質原料。我們的包裝飲用水全部源自優 using high quality raw materials. All of our packaged drinking water
質天然水源，含天然的礦物元素。我們擁有 products are sourced from quality natural water sources containing
領先的裝備水平、製造能力、強大的供應鏈 natural mineral elements. We possess industry-leading equipment,
管理能力和嚴格的質量保障體系。 manufacturing capabilities, strong supply chain management
capabilities and rigorous quality assurance systems.
我們建立了包裝飲用水和飲料的雙引擎發展 We have established dual growth engines, packaged drinking water
格局，擁有覆蓋各省市及縣級行政區域的全 and beverage and set up a nationwide sales network covering all
國性銷售網絡。我們實行多品牌戰略，具有 provinces, municipalities and prefecture-level administrative divisions
長遠的品牌規劃和強大的品牌傳播能力，鑄 across China. We implement a multi-brand strategy, have long-term
就了中國軟飲料的超級品牌。 visions for our brands and strong brand communication capabilities,
and have built a super brand in the PRC soft beverage market.
2
農夫山泉股份有限公司 NONGFU SPRING CO., LTD. 年報 2020 Annual Report

DEFINITIONS
「年度股東大會」 本公司將於2021年6月25日（星期五）召開的年度股東大會
“AGM” the annual general meeting of the Company to be held on Friday, June 25, 2021
「公司章程」或「章程」 本公司於上市日期生效的公司章程（經修訂）
“Articles of Association” the articles of association of the Company, as amended, which was effective on the Listing Date
or “Articles”
「基礎研發及檢測服務 本公司於2020年8月12日與養生堂及其全資附屬公司養生堂天然藥物研究所訂立的基礎研發及檢測服務框架
框架協議」 協議
“Basic R&D and Test the basic R&D and test framework agreement entered into by the Company with Yangshengtang and its
Framework Agreement” wholly-owned subsidiary, YST Natural Medicine Research on August 12, 2020
「董事會」 本公司董事會
“Board” the board of Directors of the Company
「中國」 中華人民共和國，就本年度報告而言，不包括香港、澳門及台灣
“China” or “PRC” the People’s Republic of China, excluding, for the purpose of this annual report, Hong Kong, Macau and
Taiwan
「本公司」或「農夫山泉」 農夫山泉股份有限公司，於1996年9月26日根據中國法律成立之股份有限公司
“Company” or “Nongfu Nongfu Spring Co., Ltd. (農夫山泉股份有限公司), a joint stock company with limited liabilities established
Spring” under the laws of the PRC on September 26, 1996
「公司法」或「中國公司法」 中華人民共和國公司法，經不時修訂、補充或以其他方式修改
“Company Law” or Company Law of the People’s Republic of China (中華人民共和國公司法), as amended, supplemented or
“PRC Company Law” otherwise modified from time to time
「控股股東」 具有《上市規則》所賦予的涵義，指鍾睒睒先生及養生堂
“Controlling Shareholder(s)” has the meaning ascribed under the Listing Rules and refers to Mr. Zhong Shanshan and Yangshengtang
「《企業管治守則》」 《上市規則》附錄十四《企業管治守則》及《企業管治報告》
“Corporate Governance Corporate Governance Code and Corporate Governance Report in Appendix 14 to the Listing Rules
Code”
「董事」 本公司董事
“Director(s)” director(s) of the Company
「內資股」 本公司股本中每股面值人民幣0.1元的普通股，以人民幣認購並繳足
“Domestic Shares” ordinary Shares in the share capital of the Company with a nominal value of RMB0.1 each, which are
subscribed for and paid up in Renminbi
「全球發售」 香港公開發售及國際發售
“Global Offering” the Hong Kong Public Offering and the International Offering
「本集團」或「我們」 本公司及其附屬公司（或視文義所需，指本公司及其任何一家或多家附屬公司），或倘文義有所需，則就本公
“Group”, “the Group”, 司成為現有附屬公司的控股公司前的期間而言的該等附屬公司，猶如其於相關時間為本公司的附屬公司
“we” or “us” the Company and its subsidiaries (or the Company and any one or more of its subsidiaries, as the content
may require), or where the context so requires, in respect of the periods before the Company became the
holding company of its present subsidiaries, such subsidiaries as if they were subsidiaries of the Company at
the relevant time
「H股」 本公司股本中每股面值人民幣0.1元的境外上市外資股，以港元買賣，並於香港聯交所上市
“H Share(s)” overseas listed foreign Shares in the share capital of the Company with a nominal value of RMB0.1 each,
which are traded in HK dollars and are listed on the Hong Kong Stock Exchange
「杭州友福」 杭州友福企業管理有限公司，於2009年12月14日根據中國法律成立之有限責任公司，截至最後可行日期，
“Hangzhou Youfu” 為養生堂的股東並由鍾睒睒先生全資持有
Hangzhou Youfu Enterprise Management Company Limited (杭州友福企業管理有限公司), a limited
liability company established under the laws of the PRC on December 14, 2009, which is a shareholder of
Yangshengtang and wholly owned by Mr. Zhong Shanshan as of the Latest Practicable Date
「香港」 中國香港特別行政區
“Hong Kong” or “HK” the Hong Kong Special Administrative Region of the PRC

人力資源與酬金政策 HUMAN RESOURCES AND REMUNERATION
POLICY
於2020年12月31日，本集團員工總人數為 As at December 31, 2020, the total number of employees of the
19,079人。2020年本集團加強了企業文化 Group was 19,079. In 2020, the Group strengthened corporate
培訓及基層管理培訓。根據不同階段、不同 culture training and grassroots management training. According
崗位的員工發展需求，本集團設計並開發了 to the development needs of employees at different stages and
差異化的學習方案，以理論學習結合實際操 positions, the Group has designed and developed differentiated
作，構建完整的培訓體系，確保整體員工的 learning programs. Combining theoretical learning with practical
培訓效果，從多個層面開展培訓計劃的落實 operations, the Group has built a compressive training system

適的會計政策、採用合適的會計準則、作出 year. For the purpose of the preparation of the financial statements
審慎合理的判斷及評估、以及確保帳目以持 for the Reporting Period, the Directors have selected appropriate
續經營基準編製。董事確認，本集團財務報 accounting policies, adopted applicable accounting principles, made
表之編製乃符合法定要求及適用會計準則。 judgments and assessments that are prudent and reasonable and

風險管理及內部監控 RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL
董事會已按照《企業管治守則》第C.2段的要 The Board has established a risk management and internal control
求建立了風險管理及內部監控系統，負責對 system in accordance with the requirements of paragraph C.2 of the
本公司的風險管理及內部監控系統的充分性 Corporate Governance Code, which is responsible for independently
和有效性進行獨立審核，持續監督並每年 reviewing the adequacy and effectiveness of the risk management
至少檢討一次其運行的有效性。該系統旨在 and internal control system of the Company, and continuously
管理而非消除未能達成業務目標的風險，促 monitoring and reviewing the effectiveness of its operation once a
進有效及高效運營，合理保證財務報告可靠 year. The system aims to manage rather than eliminate the risk of
性及遵守適用法律及法規以及保障本集團資 failure to achieve business objectives, promote effective and efficient
產。董事會只能就不會有重大的失實陳述或 operations, reasonably ensure the reliability of financial reports and
損失作出合理而非絕對的保證。 comply with applicable laws and regulations and protect the assets
of the Group. The Board can only give reasonable but not absolute
assurance that there will be no material misrepresentation or loss.

Organization System

足夠的資源、員工資歷及經驗、足夠的培訓 management and internal control organization system which includes
課程和有關預算，本集團建立了完善的風險 the Board, the Audit Committee, the Supervisory Committee, the

辦公室、審計稽查中心、法務部以及其他各 to ensure that the Group has sufficient resources, qualified and
部門。本集團各部門為風險管理和內部監控 experienced staffs, training courses and related budget for risk
第一道防綫；本集團董事會辦公室和管理層 management and internal audit. The various departments of the
為第二道防綫；審計稽查中心、法務部、審 Group are the first line of defense for risk management and internal
計委員會及監事會為第三道防綫。董事會對 control; the office of the Board and the management of the Group

風險管理和內部監控工作開展 Implementation of Risk Management and Internal Control
本集團每年對風險管理及內部監控系統的有 The Group reviews the effectiveness of the risk management and
效性進行檢討，根據內部環境、風險評估、 internal control systems every year and assesses all important
控制活動、信息與溝通及內部監督等內部監 monitoring aspects including supervision on financial, operation and
控元素，對所有重要的監控方面，包括財務 compliance affairs, etc. based on the elements of internal control
監控、運作監控及合規監控等進行評估。 such as the internal environment, risk assessment, control activities,
information and communication as well as internal supervision.
經過風險評估，公司2020年度重大風險主 After risk assessment, the Company’s major risks in 2020 mainly
要涉及業務風險、財務風險、合規風險和內 involve business risks, financial risks, compliance risks, inside
幕消息及內部監控風險。 information and internal control risks.
業務風險：本集團面臨的業務風險主要包括 Business risks: The business risks the Group mainly faces include
聲譽風險、投資及收購風險、公司責任及可 reputation risks, investment and acquisition risks, and corporate
持續發展風險。董事會定期檢討本集團的發 responsibility and sustainability risks. The Board regularly reviews
展策略、業務計劃、財務業績及主要的表現 the development strategies, business plans, financial results, and
指標，以確保業務風險已受控制及管理，並 key performance indicators of the Group to ensure that the business
可識別出潛在風險。 risks are controlled and managed, and potential risks can be
identified.
財務風險：本集團已採納財務風險管理政 Financial risks: The Group has adopted financial risk management
策，以控制本集團的財務風險，例如稅務風 policies to control the Group’s financial risk exposure, such as
險、貨幣風險及財務報告風險。而且，董事 taxation risks, currency risks and financial reporting risks. Also, the
會在本集團內財務部門協助下，定期監督財 Board monitors the financial results and key operating statistics
務業績及主要營運數據。 with the assistance of the Group’s internal finance department on a
regular basis.

董事會報告
REPORT OF THE DIRECTORS
董事會欣然提呈其報告連同本集團截至 The Board is pleased to present its report together with the audited
2020年12月31日止年度之經審核綜合財務 consolidated financial statements of the Group for the year ended
報表。 December 31, 2020.
主要業務 PRINCIPAL BUSINESS
本集團是中國包裝飲用水及飲料的龍頭企 The Group is a leader in the packaged drinking water and beverage
業，致力於向消費者提供高質量的產品。本 business in the PRC with devotion to providing consumers with high-
集團的主要產品覆蓋包裝飲用水、茶飲料、 quality products. The Group’s major products include packaged
功能飲料及果汁飲料等類別。 drinking water, tea beverage, functional beverage and juice
beverage.
自1996年成立以來，本集團即確保「每一滴 Since its establishment in 1996, the Group has adhered to the
農夫山泉都有它的源頭」。本集團的包裝飲 principle that “every drop of Nongfu Spring has its source” 「( 每一
用水產品全部源自天然水源。我們已在中國 滴農夫山泉都有它的源頭」). The Group sources all of packaged
各不同區域佈局了十大優質水源地，全面覆 drinking water products from natural water sources. We have
蓋了全國市場供應。該十大水源所在地生態 obtained access to ten quality natural water sources in different
環境卓越，水質優異。本集團對優質天然原 regions of China which cover the entire national market supply. The
水做包括過濾及殺菌在內的必要處理。我們 ten water sources are all located in excellent ecological environments
的包裝飲用水產品含有鉀、鈉、鈣、鎂、偏 and have outstanding water quality. The Group treats the quality
硅酸等對人體有益的礦物元素。 natural water with the necessary process, including filtration
and sterilization. Our packaged drinking water products contain
the natural minerals that are beneficial to human body, such as
potassium, sodium, calcium, magnesium and metasilicate.

董事會報告 REPORT OF THE DIRECTORS
本集團建立了包裝飲用水和飲料的雙引擎發 The Group has established dual growth engines, packaged drinking
展格局，既有利於增強我們的市場競爭能力 water and beverage, which enhance our competitive advantage
又能有效降低業績波動風險。本集團實行多 and reduce the risks of fluctuating operating results. The Group
品牌戰略，具有長遠的品牌規劃和強大的品 implements a multi-brand strategy, has long-term visions for our
牌傳播能力，鑄就了中國軟飲料的超級品 brands and strong brand communication capabilities, and has built a
牌。 super brand in the PRC soft beverage market.

本公司的可供分派利潤為其根據(i)中國會計 determining distributable profit, the distributable profit of the Company
準則及規則；及(ii)國際或者境外上市地會計 shall be the lower of its profit after tax determined in accordance
準則計算所得的稅後利潤兩者中較低者。 with: (i) the PRC accounting standards and regulations; and (ii) the
international standards or the accounting standards of the place(s)
outside the PRC where the shares of the Company are listed.

Connected Transactions

按經常性及持續基準繼續進行，董事認為， are expected to continue a recurring and continuing basis, our
遵守上述公告規定乃不切實際，將使我們招 Directors consider that compliance with the above announcement
致不必要的行政費用，並對我們造成不必要 requirements will be impractical, will incur unnecessary administrative
的負擔。 costs for us, and will be unduly burdensome to us.
因此，我們已向香港聯交所申請，而香港聯 Accordingly, we have applied to the Hong Kong Stock Exchange
交所根據《上市規則》第14A.105條已授予我 for, and the Hong Kong Stock Exchange has granted, a waiver to
們就上述不獲豁免持續關連交易遵守公告規 us under Rule 14A.105 of the Listing Rules from compliance with
定的豁免。 the announcement requirements in respect of the above non-fully
exempt continuing connected transactions.

度的持續關連交易項下具體協議的簽訂及執 of the implementation agreements under the continuing connected

NOTES TO FINANCIAL STATEMENTS
2020年12月31日
31 December 2020
1. 公司及集團資料 1. CORPORATE AND GROUP INFORMATION
農夫山泉股份有限公司於2001年6月 Nongfu Spring Co., Ltd. was incorporated and registered in
27日在中華人民共和國（「中國」）註冊 the People’s Republic of China (“PRC”) on 27 June 2001. On 8
成立及登記。於2020年9月8日，本公 September 2020, the Company was listed on the Main Board
司於香港聯合交易所有限公司（「香港 of the Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “HKSE”)
聯交所」）主板上市（股份代號：9633. (stock code: 9633. HK). The address of the registered office is
HK）。註冊辦事處地址為浙江省杭州 No. 181, Geyazhuang Road, Xihu District, Hangzhou, Zhejiang
市西湖區葛衙莊181號。 Province.

2.1 編製基準 2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION
該等綜合財務報表乃根據國際財務報 These consolidated financial statements have been prepared
告準則（「國際財務報告準則」）編製， in accordance with International Financial Reporting Standards
當中包括國際會計準則理事會（「國際 (“IFRSs”), which comprise standards and interpretations
會計準則理事會」）批准的準則及詮 approved by the International Accounting Standards Board
釋，及國際會計準則理事會批准的國 (the “IASB”), and International Accounting Standards and
際會計準則及常務詮釋委員會詮釋以 Standing Interpretations Committee interpretations approved
及香港公司條例之披露規定。本集團 by the International Accounting Standards Committee and
於編製截至2020年及2019年12月31日 the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies
止整個財政年度的財務報表時已提早 Ordinance. All IFRSs effective for the accounting period
採納於2020年1月1日開始的會計期間 commencing from 1 January 2020, together with the relevant
生效的所有國際財務報告準則，連同 transitional provisions, had been early adopted by the Group
相關過渡性條文。 in the preparation of the financial statements throughout the
financial years ended 31 December 2020 and 2019.
除衍生金融工具按公平值計量外，綜 The consolidated financial statements have been prepared
合財務報表乃按歷史成本法編製。除 under the historical cost convention, except for derivative
另有註明者外，該等財務報表以人民 financial instruments which have been measured at fair value.
幣（「人民幣」）呈列，且所有數值均調 These financial statements are presented in Renminbi (“RMB”)
整至最近的千元單位。 and all values are rounded to the nearest thousand except
when otherwise indicated.
綜合基準 Basis of consolidation
綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公 The consolidated financial statements include the financial
司（統稱「本集團」）於截至2020年12月 statements of the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively
31日止年度的財務報表。附屬公司為 referred to as the “Group”) for the year ended 31 December
本公司直接或間接控制的實體（包括結 2020. A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity),
構性實體）。當本集團對參與被投資方 directly or indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control
業務的可變回報承擔風險或享有權利 is achieved when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to
以及能透過其權力影響被投資方的回 variable returns from its involvement with the investee and has
報時（即賦予本集團現有能力主導被投 the ability to affect those returns through its power over the
資方相關活動的既存權利），即取得控 investee (i.e., existing rights that give the Group the current
制權。 ability to direct the relevant activities of the investee).
倘本公司直接或間接擁有少於被投資 When the Company has, directly or indirectly, less than a
方過半數投票或類似權利，則本集團 majority of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the Group
於評估其是否對被投資方擁有權力時 considers all relevant facts and circumstances in assessing
會考慮一切相關事實及情況，包括： whether it has power over an investee, including:
(a) 與被投資方其他投票權持有人的 (a) the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders
合約安排； of the investee;

公平值計量 Fair value measurement
本集團計量各報告期末的衍生金融工 The Group measures its derivative financial instruments and
具及股本投資。公平值為市場參與者 equity investments at the end of each reporting period. Fair
於計量日期在有序交易中出售資產所 value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or
收取或轉讓負債所支付的價格。公平 paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between
值計量乃基於假設出售資產或轉讓負 market participants at the measurement date. The fair value
債的交易於資產或負債的主要市場或 measurement is based on the presumption that the transaction
（在無主要市場的情況下）資產或負債 to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in the
的最有利市場進行。主要或最有利市 principal market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a
場必須為本集團可進入的市場。資產 principal market, in the most advantageous market for the asset
或負債的公平值乃採用市場參與者為 or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market
資產或負債定價時所用的假設計量， must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset
並假設市場參與者依照彼等的最佳經 or a liability is measured using the assumptions that market
濟利益行事。 participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,
assuming that market participants act in their economic best
interest.

物業、廠房及設備及折舊 Property, plant and equipment and depreciation

賬。物業、廠房及設備項目的成本包 any impairment losses. The cost of an item of property, plant
括其購買價及任何使資產達致其運作 and equipment comprises its purchase price and any directly
狀況及地點作擬定用途的直接應佔成 attributable costs of bringing the asset to its working condition
本。 and location for its intended use.

生的開支（如維修及保養）一般於產生 equipment have been put into operation, such as repairs and
期間自損益表扣除。在符合確認條件 maintenance, is normally charged to the statement of profit or
的情況下，重大檢查支出會於資產的 loss in the period in which it is incurred. In situations where the

替換，本集團會確認該等部分為有特 a replacement. Where significant parts of property, plant and
定可使用年期的個別資產，並對其作 equipment are required to be replaced at intervals, the Group
出相應折舊。 recognises such parts as individual assets with specific useful
lives and depreciates them accordingly.
折舊乃按各項物業、廠房及設備的估 Depreciation is calculated on the straight-line basis to write off
計可使用年期以直線法撇銷其成本至 the cost of each item of property, plant and equipment to its
其剩餘價值計算。就此採用的主要年 residual value over its estimated useful life. The principal annual
率如下： rates used for this purpose are as follows:

使用年期不同，則該項目的成本按合 different useful lives, the cost of that item is allocated on a
理基準分配至各部分，而各部分將分 reasonable basis among the parts and each part is depreciated

租賃－估算增量借款利率 Leases – Estimating the incremental borrowing rate
本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的 The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in
利率，因此，使用增量借款利率（「增 a lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing rate
量借款利率」）計量租賃負債。增量借 (“IBR”) to measure lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest
款利率為本集團於類似經濟環境中為 that the Group would have to pay to borrow over a similar
取得與使用權資產價值相近之資產， term, and with a similar security, the funds necessary to obtain
而以類似抵押品與類似期間借入所需 an asset of a similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar
資金應支付之利率。因此，增量借款 economic environment. The IBR therefore reflects what the
利率反映了本集團「應支付」的利率， Group “would have to pay”, which requires estimation when
當無可觀察的利率時（如就並無訂立融 no observable rates are available (such as for subsidiaries that
資交易之附屬公司而言）或當須對利率 do not enter into financing transactions) or when it needs to
進行調整以反映租賃之條款及條件時 be adjusted to reflect the terms and conditions of the lease
（如當租賃並非以附屬公司之功能貨幣 (for example, when leases are not in the subsidiary’s functional
訂立時），則須作出利率估計。當可觀 currency). The Group estimates the IBR using observable
察輸入數據可用時，本集團使用可觀 inputs (such as market interest rates) when available and is
察輸入數據（如市場利率）估算增量借 required to make certain entity-specific estimates (such as the
款利率並須作出若干實體特定的估計 subsidiary’s stand-alone credit rating).
（例如附屬公司的獨立信貸利率）。

本集團採用簡化法計提國際財務報告 The Group applies the simplified approach to providing for
準則第9號所規定的預期信貸虧損，其 expected credit losses prescribed by IFRS 9, which permits the
允許就所有貿易應收款項使用全期預 use of the lifetime expected credit loss provision for all trade
期信貸虧損撥備。 receivables.
應收票據為獲銀行於期限內無條件接 Bills receivable are bank acceptance bills that are
納的銀行承兌票據，且並不會就應收 unconditionally accepted by banks within the maturity period,
票據減值計提虧損撥備。 and there is no loss allowance for impairment of bills receivable.
年末使用撥備矩陣進行減值分析，以 An impairment analysis is performed at year end using a
計量貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損。 provision matrix to measure expected credit losses for trade
撥備率乃基於具有類似虧損模式的多 receivables. The provision rates are based on days past due
個客戶分部組別的逾期日數釐定。該 for groupings of various customer segments with similar loss
計算反映了概率加權結果，貨幣時間 patterns. The calculation reflects the probability-weighted
價值以及年末可獲得的關於過往事 outcome, the time value of money and reasonable and
件、當前狀況及未來經濟狀況預測的 supportable information that is available at year end about past
合理及可支持的資料。 events, current conditions and forecasts of future economic
conditions.
當有資訊表明交易對手處於嚴重財務 The Group writes off trade receivables when there is
困難時，且並無合理預期收回時，本 information indicating that the counterparty is in severe financial
集團將註銷其貿易應收款項。本集團 difficulties and there is no realistic prospect of recovery. The
亦並在合適情況下考慮法律意見，例 Group also takes into account legal advice where appropriate,
如交易對手已被清盤或已進入破產程 e.g. when the counterparty has been placed under liquidation
序，以較早者為準。 or has entered into bankruptcy proceedings, whichever occurs
sooner.

(i) 法定儲備 (i) Statutory reserve
根據中華人民共和國公司法，中 In accordance with the Company Law of the People’s
國公司須將法定除稅後利潤的 Republic of China, the companies in the PRC are
10%轉撥至法定儲備，直至儲備 required to allocate 10% of the statutory after tax profits
的累計總額達到公司註冊資本的 to the statutory reserve until the cumulative total of
50%。待中國有關部門批准後， the reserve reaches 50% of the companies’ registered
法定儲備可用於抵銷任何累計虧 capital. Subject to the approval of the relevant PRC
損或增加公司的註冊資本。法定 authorities, the statutory reserve may be used to offset
儲備不可用於向中國附屬公司股 any accumulated losses or increase the registered capital
東分派股息。 of the companies. The statutory reserve is not available
for dividend distribution to shareholders of the PRC
subsidiaries.
(ii) 資本儲備 (ii) Capital reserve
本集團的資本儲備指最終控股公 The capital reserve of the Group represents the
司及股東的貢獻及代價超出所收 contribution from the ultimate holding company and
購非控股權益的賬面值的部分。 shareholders and the excess of the consideration over
the carrying amount of the non-controlling interests
acquired.

41. 金融風險管理目標及政策（續） 41. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES
AND POLICIES (continued)
資本管理 Capital management
本集團資本管理的主要目標為確保本 The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are
集團持續經營的能力以及維持穩健的 to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern
資本比率，以支持其業務及為股東爭 and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its
取最大價值。 business and maximise shareholders’ value.
本集團考慮經濟狀況變化及相關資產 The Group manages its capital structure and makes
的風險特徵來管理其資本架構並作出 adjustments to it in light of changes in economic conditions
相應調整。為維持或調整資本架構， and the risk characteristics of the underlying assets. To
本集團或會調整支付予股東的股息、 maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust
退回股本予股東或發行新股。本集團 the dividend payment to shareholders, return capital to
不受任何外部資本限制的約束。年 shareholders or issue new shares. The Group is not subject to
內，本集團的資本管理目標、政策或 any externally imposed capital requirements. No changes were
程序未發生變化。 made in the objectives, policies or processes for managing
capital during the year.
本集團採用資本負債比率監控資本， The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is
該比率按債務除以經調整資本計算。 debt divided by the adjusted capital. Debt includes interest-
債務包括計息借貸以及租賃負債。資 bearing borrowings and lease liabilities. Capital includes equity
本包括母公司擁有人應佔權益。於各 attributable to owners of the parent. The gearing ratios as at
